- **skill-ab-eval**: Run an A/B usefulness check on an agent-skill — does it actually change agent behavior? Use when asked to "check if a skill is useful", "A/B test a skill", "measure a skill's effect", "skill 有用性チェック", or to validate a skill before/after editing it.
